{"etymology_number":"5","forms":[{"form":"rakes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"raking","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"raked","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"raked","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"A mast rakes aft.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To incline (something) from a perpendicular direction."],"synonyms":[{"word":"slope"}],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to watercraft.","To provide (the bow or stern of a watercraft) with a rake (“a slant that causes it to extend beyond the keel”)."],"tags":["transitive"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to watercraft.","Of a watercraft: to have a rake at its bow or stern."],"tags":["intransitive","rare"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹeɪk/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":32256,"upstream_offset":218813485,"upstream_record_sha256":"6ad5026425b96a5e2ba5c4324361f9ad48f8eef3e872dbd43b36e75b56bbde59","word":"rake"}
{"etymology_number":"5","forms":[{"form":"rakes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A divergence from the horizontal or perpendicular; a slant, a slope."]},{"glosses":["In full, angle of rake or rake angle: the angle between the edge or face of a tool (especially a cutting tool) and a plane (usually one perpendicular to the object that the tool is being applied to)."],"tags":["specifically"]},{"glosses":["The direction of slip during the movement of a fault, measured within the fault plane."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to watercraft.","A slant that causes the bow or stern of a watercraft to extend beyond the keel; also, the upper part of the bow or stern that extends beyond the keel."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to watercraft.","A slant of some other part of a watercraft (such as a funnel or mast) away from the perpendicular, usually towards the stern."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["The sloped edge of a roof at or adjacent to the first or last rafter."],"topics":["business","construction","manufacturing","roofing"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɹeɪk/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":32257,"upstream_offset":218822237,"upstream_record_sha256":"56cde2082dc668d1fbbaa48ae857914aa7a11352a1f6ed8e4f4604579cb6ff16","word":"rake"}
